W receives a noisy harmful-intent diagnostic before observations. IG pools a common latent agenda over independent source truths; outsider library has strength 0.5, insider library includes 1 with honesty prior 0.5+b. This library/prior contrast is not a human mechanism. Partial tag loss is compared with its no-switch counterfactual.
